#### 📋 DFCT1789246: FanStack Persona Resonance & Voice Restoration (DFCT1789246)
*   **System ID:** `b94c03f0a36544dda1436dcd10237f53`
*   **Full Description:**
> ﻿Work Order: FanStack Persona Resonance & Voice Restoration (DFCT1789246)
> Ticket ID: DFCT1789246 (WO-2026-126)
> Priority: ⚡ P1 — High-Priority Persona Remediation
> Status: Staged for Execution
> Assigned To: antigravity
> Target Node: Clio (Sovereign OS Core - 100.73.155.70)
> Ecosystem Location: Sovereign Sports Chatbot Engine (19_Sovereign_Sports/scripts/fanstack_chatbots.py / the_skew_chatbots.py)
> Parent Sprint: Sovereign CMDB Registry and Network Stabilization (Sprint ID 3)
> 1. Context & Objective
> During recent user acceptance testing (UAT) and a system audit of the active FanStack watch party rooms, the Pilot noted a severe loss of comedic resonance, "unhinged mania," and gallows humor across our core AI advocate personas. Most notably, @barf has lost his signature Queens-native frustration, delivering sanitized, polite, and generic sports fan boilerplate (such as "Bullpen spending is high but results are pending. We have resources, but we need structure. It is a long season.").
> 
> 
> An deep-trace audit of the prompt-assembling pipeline (Document Reference: AUD-2026-BARF-RESONANCE) identified the root cause as Cognitive Dampening via Prompt Bloat. Over the last few migration cycles, the highly descriptive 05/15/26 historical prompts were over-engineered and wrapped in restrictive compliance guardrails, strict statistical grounding blocks ("Strict Grounding Rule"), and safety venue constraints. This flattened the advocate's emotional peaks and neutralized their historical reference memory banks (e.g., Johan Santana's no-hitter toll, Tom Glavine's 2007 collapse, Bobby Bonilla Day, and Jeurys Familia's bullpen betrayal).
> 
> 
> This work order directs antigravity to refactor the chatbot generator compilers, strip away the restrictive context-dilution filters for characters flagged as "agitator" or "pacer," and restore @barf's high-entropy historical system prompt to its absolute peak.
> 2. Technical Specifications & Implementation Steps
> A. Refactoring the Chatbot prompt compilers (fanstack_chatbots.py & the_skew_chatbots.py)
> Modify the prompt assembly function to check the advocate's active classification profile. If a persona's cadence is registered as agitator or their u_entropy_level (or Boggs Reactivity) is greater than or equal to 4:
> 
>
